.. _class_NetwMultiplayer:

NetwMultiplayer
===============

**Inherits:** :godot:`MultiplayerAPIExtension`

The :godot:`MultiplayerAPI` implementation for a Networked session.

.. rst-class:: classref-introduction-group

Description
-----------

Use :ref:`Netw.of()<class_Netw_method_of>` to get the session for a node. Most game code should use :ref:`Netw<class_Netw>` and the handle classes instead of calling this class directly.

::

    var api := Netw.of(self)
    api.entity_despawn(route)
    api.scene_admit(scene, participant)

\ Method prefixes identify their subsystem and related API class:

- ``scene_*``: :ref:`NetwSceneHandle<class_NetwSceneHandle>`\

- ``interest_*``: :ref:`NetwInterestHandle<class_NetwInterestHandle>`\

- ``session_*``: :ref:`NetwSessionHandle<class_NetwSessionHandle>`\

- ``clock_*``: :ref:`NetwClockHandle<class_NetwClockHandle>`\

- ``predict_*``: :ref:`NetwPredictionHandle<class_NetwPredictionHandle>`\

- ``entity_*``, ``liveness_*``, and ``spawn_*``: :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>`\

- ``table_*``: :ref:`NetwRecordTable<class_NetwRecordTable>`\

- ``schema_*``: :ref:`NetwSchema<class_NetwSchema>`\

- ``lagcomp_*``: :ref:`NetwAction<class_NetwAction>`\

- ``peer_*``: :ref:`NetwParticipant<class_NetwParticipant>`\

- ``display_*``: :ref:`NetwDisplayHandle<class_NetwDisplayHandle>`\

- ``endpoint_*`` and ``embed_*``: :ref:`NetwConnectHandle<class_NetwConnectHandle>`\

- ``transport_*``: :ref:`NetwTransport<class_NetwTransport>`\

- ``rpc_*`` and ``sync_*``: :ref:`NetwMemberConfig<class_NetwMemberConfig>`\

- ``property_set_*``: :ref:`NetwPropertySet<class_NetwPropertySet>`\

- ``service_*``: :ref:`NetwService<class_NetwService>`\

- ``persist_*``: :ref:`NetwDatabase<class_NetwDatabase>`\

- ``auth_*``: :ref:`NetwAuthFlow<class_NetwAuthFlow>`\

- ``stats_*`` and ``attribution_*``: :ref:`NetwPredictStats<class_NetwPredictStats>`\

Methods beginning with an underscore are virtual extension points. Override one in a :godot:`Script` and call its corresponding ``_default`` method to use the default implementation.

::

    extends NetwMultiplayer

    func _predict_consume(depth: int, buffer: int) -> int:
        if depth > 8:
            return NetwPredict.CONSUME_ACTION_REPLAY
        return predict_consume_default(depth, buffer)

\ Install the subclass with :ref:`MultiplayerTree.api_script<class_MultiplayerTree_property_api_script>`. Call ``_default`` directly instead of using ``super``.

.. rst-class:: classref-descriptions-group

Signals
-------

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_after_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_after_tick**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`, tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_after_tick>`

Announced by the session clock at the end of each tick, after game logic. The clock announces on the session rather than on itself, so a consumer holding the session never has to hold the clock and there is one place a tick can be subscribed to.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_after_tick_loop:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_after_tick_loop**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_after_tick_loop>`

Announced by the session clock once after :ref:`clock_physics_step()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_physics_step>`'s tick loop finishes, whether or not it ran a tick. Not announced by :ref:`clock_step()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_step>`, which is a step rather than a frame.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_before_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_before_tick**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`, tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_before_tick>`

Announced by the session clock at the start of each tick, before game logic.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_before_tick_loop:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_before_tick_loop**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_before_tick_loop>`

Announced by the session clock once before :ref:`clock_physics_step()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_physics_step>`'s tick loop runs, whether or not it will run a tick. Not announced by :ref:`clock_step()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_step>`, which is a step rather than a frame.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_configured:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_configured**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_configured>`

Announced once, after this session consumed its clock configuration and armed the automatic pump. A listener connected afterwards has already missed it and reads :ref:`clock_is_configured()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_configured>` instead.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_on_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_on_tick**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`, tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_on_tick>`

Announced by the session clock during each tick. Primary simulation logic subscribes here.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_pong_received:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_pong_received**\ (\ data\: :godot:`Dictionary`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_pong_received>`

Announced by :ref:`clock_ingest_pong()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_ingest_pong>` once a round trip :ref:`clock_send_ping()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_send_ping>` opened has closed and the clock has calibrated against it. ``data`` is that sample, which is the only place the raw round trip is readable at all.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴rtt_raw                     float   this sample's round trip, in seconds
    ┠╴rtt_avg                     float   the running average round trip
    ┠╴rtt_jitter                  float   the running jitter estimate
    ┠╴diff                        int     ticks the calibration moved this peer
    ┠╴tick                        int     the tick after calibrating
    ┠╴display_offset              float   the offset the display is running
    ┠╴recommended_display_offset  float   the offset this sample recommends
    ┠╴is_stable                   bool    whether jitter is under threshold
    ┖╴is_synchronized             bool    whether a calibration has ever landed

\ :ref:`clock_stability_changed<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_stability_changed>` and :ref:`clock_synchronized<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_synchronized>` announce the transitions of the last two, so a listener that only wants those does not read this at all.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_stability_changed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_stability_changed**\ (\ is_stable\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_stability_changed>`

Announced by the session clock when :ref:`clock_is_stable()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_stable>` flips, so a listener sees the transition rather than having to poll for it.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_synchronized:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_synchronized**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_synchronized>`

Announced by the session clock once, when its first calibration lands. A later pong recalibrates without re-announcing.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_tickrate_mismatch:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_tickrate_mismatch**\ (\ peer_id\: :godot:`int`, their_tickrate\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_tickrate_mismatch>`

Announced when a handshake reply reports a tickrate this session does not run and :ref:`clock_set_mismatch_action()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_mismatch_action>` holds :ref:`MISMATCH_ACTION_SIGNAL<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_MISMATCH_ACTION_SIGNAL>`, which is the setting that hands the decision to the game instead of taking one. ``peer_id`` is the peer that replied and ``their_tickrate`` is the rate it reported.

Neither other action announces it. :ref:`MISMATCH_ACTION_WARN<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_MISMATCH_ACTION_WARN>` only logs the mismatch, and :ref:`MISMATCH_ACTION_DISCONNECT<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_MISMATCH_ACTION_DISCONNECT>` closes the peer.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_embed_phase_changed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**embed_phase_changed**\ (\ phase\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_embed_phase_changed>`

Emitted when :ref:`embed_phase()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_phase>` advances, carrying the phase just entered. The installing embedding is the only caller that advances it, through :ref:`embed_settle()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_settle>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_endpoint_added:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**endpoint_added**\ (\ endpoint\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_endpoint_added>`

A row entered :ref:`endpoint_list()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_list>`. A row already known announces nothing, because its key is the pair it always was.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_endpoint_removed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**endpoint_removed**\ (\ endpoint\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_endpoint_removed>`

A row left :ref:`endpoint_list()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_list>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_endpoint_updated:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**endpoint_updated**\ (\ endpoint\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_endpoint_updated>`

A probe or a listing returned for this row. What changed is read back through :ref:`endpoint_get_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_get_param>` and :ref:`endpoint_get_state()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_get_state>`, so a browser redraws one row rather than the list.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_dead:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**entity_dead**\ (\ route\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_dead>`

Emitted once when this session learns that ``route`` has ended. The route reads :ref:`ENTITY_STATE_DEAD<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_DEAD>`, its body services are released, and the wrapper has already left the live index, so :ref:`entity_get_view()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_get_view>` returns ``null`` for ``route`` here and for the rest of the cycle.

A materialized entity emits :ref:`NetwEntity.despawned<class_NetwEntity_signal_despawned>` before this, and that per-entity completion is what a tracked entity with no route gets instead. A move emits neither, and a receiving peer that lost only its copy emits :ref:`entity_hidden<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_hidden>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_hidden:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**entity_hidden**\ (\ route\: :godot:`int`, entity\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_hidden>`

Emitted when this receiving peer loses its copy of ``route``, carrying the old :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>` as ``entity``. The route reads :ref:`ENTITY_STATE_ABSENT<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_ABSENT>` afterwards and the entity has already left the live set, exactly as for :ref:`entity_dead<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_dead>`.

What survives is the identity and anything waiting on it, so a later spawn from the server binds the same handle to a new node. Listen to this rather than :ref:`entity_dead<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_dead>` for anything that should come back.

\ :ref:`NetwEntity.hidden<class_NetwEntity_signal_hidden>` fires first and carries what was released. A hide the server issued reports before the old node is freed, and a copy this peer deleted itself reports once that node is already gone. Neither changes the server's scene admission, and neither emits\ :ref:`entity_dead<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_dead>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_lingering:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**entity_lingering**\ (\ route\: :godot:`int`, entity\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_lingering>`

Emitted when an entity enters :ref:`ENTITY_STATE_LINGERING<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_LINGERING>`. Its :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>` stays resolvable, because a lingering entity has not gone yet.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_live:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**entity_live**\ (\ route\: :godot:`int`, entity\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_live>`

Emitted when a route becomes live. It carries the :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>` the session itself holds, so a listener resolves the same object the session will return with for the rest of that route's life.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_lagcomp_action_gate_fallback:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**lagcomp_action_gate_fallback**\ (\ key\: :godot:`StringName`, view_tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_lagcomp_action_gate_fallback>`

Announced when an action timed :ref:`NetwAction.TIMING_TICK_ALIGNED_STATE_READY<class_NetwAction_constant_TIMING_TICK_ALIGNED_STATE_READY>` reaches :ref:`NetwLagCompensationConfig.input_gate_deadline_ticks<class_NetwLagCompensationConfig_property_input_gate_deadline_ticks>` without the input state it was waiting for, and so executes best effort rather than waiting further. ``key`` is the action's key and ``view_tick`` the tick it was aligned to. An action the gate admits on time is not announced, and every announcement is also counted into :ref:`lagcomp_metrics()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_metrics>`'s ``gate_fallbacks``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_participant_joined:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**participant_joined**\ (\ participant\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_participant_joined>`

Emitted for every peer the session admits, this one included. A peer with no roster row has not been admitted and is never announced.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_participant_local_joined:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**participant_local_joined**\ (\ participant\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_participant_local_joined>`

Emitted when the peer being admitted is this one, ahead of :ref:`participant_joined<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_participant_joined>`, so a listener handling both sees itself arrive before it sees the roster grow.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_participant_viewport_changed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**participant_viewport_changed**\ (\ viewport\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_participant_viewport_changed>`

Emitted when :ref:`scene_participant_viewport()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_participant_viewport>` resolves to a different :godot:`SubViewport`, and ``viewport`` is ``null`` when it resolves to none. A settle that lands on what the last one did stays silent, so a listener is never told to re-adopt a display it already holds.

The settle is deferred, because the local player is assigned while its entity is still entering the tree and the viewport it lands in resolves only once that entry finishes.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_authenticating:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**peer_authenticating**\ (\ peer_id\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_authenticating>`

Republishes :godot:`SceneMultiplayer.peer_authenticating <SceneMultiplayer#class_SceneMultiplayer_signal_peer_authenticating>` from the transport this session holds. A transport the session replaces stops republishing at once.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_authentication_failed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**peer_authentication_failed**\ (\ peer_id\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_authentication_failed>`

Republishes :godot:`SceneMultiplayer.peer_authentication_failed <SceneMultiplayer#class_SceneMultiplayer_signal_peer_authentication_failed>` from the transport this session holds, after recording the rejection as a ``PEER_AUTH_FAILED`` event, so a sink reads it in the order the session decided it.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_kick_requested:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**peer_kick_requested**\ (\ requester_id\: :godot:`int`, target_id\: :godot:`int`, reason\: :godot:`String`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_kick_requested>`

Emitted when a client asks for a peer to be kicked. Heard only by a session holding peer id 1. A request that does not carry a target and a reason is dropped rather than announced against a default target.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_kicked:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**peer_kicked**\ (\ reason\: :godot:`String`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_kicked>`

Emitted when server authority kicks this peer, before the connection closes. A notice from anyone but the server is dropped.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_packet:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**peer_packet**\ (\ id\: :godot:`int`, packet\: :godot:`PackedByteArray`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_packet>`

Emitted for a datagram this session did not frame, so a game's own byte traffic reaches it over the same transport. A datagram that is this session's own and cannot be read is never announced here.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_predict_owner_divergence:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**predict_owner_divergence**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`, entry\: :godot:`int`, attribution\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_predict_owner_divergence>`

Announced on server authority when an owner's claimed post-state for a transition disagrees with the state authority itself reached, so a diagnostic sink hears the disagreement the tick it is judged rather than reading a counter later. ``peer`` is the controller of the entity seated in the slot, ``entry`` is the transition judged, and ``attribution`` is the :ref:`Attribution<enum_NetwPredictJournal_Attribution>` the comparison charged the divergence to.

A claim that matched is counted and not announced, and so is one the comparison could charge to nothing, which leaves :ref:`NetwPredictJournal.UNKNOWN<class_NetwPredictJournal_constant_UNKNOWN>` out of this signal's vocabulary. Both are counted either way, through :ref:`NetwPredictStats.client_fp_verified<class_NetwPredictStats_property_client_fp_verified>` and :ref:`NetwPredictStats.client_mismatches<class_NetwPredictStats_property_client_mismatches>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_activated:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**scene_activated**\ (\ scene\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_activated>`

Emitted by :ref:`scene_activate()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_activate>` for the container a caller made current, once per activation and never for a rejection. Reusing a container that was already live still announces, because the signal reports what the session now presents and not whether a spawn happened.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_despawned:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**scene_despawned**\ (\ scene\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_despawned>`

Emitted for a scene the session has dropped, after its rows are gone and everything waiting on the change has run, so a listener reading membership sees the session without it. Dropping a node the session never held still announces, because the scene leaving is the fact being reported and having nothing to drop is not a rejection.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_entity_moved:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**scene_entity_moved**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`Object`, from\: :godot:`Object`, to\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_entity_moved>`

Emitted for a move that completed, after the mover's membership and saved rows have settled and before the move's :ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` resolves, so a listener and an awaiting caller read the same session. A rejected move never arrives and is never announced.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_live:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**scene_live**\ (\ scene\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_live>`

Emitted for the scene a newly live route belongs to. The handle is the scene's own, so a node coming live inside a scene announces the scene and not itself.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_local_changed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**scene_local_changed**\ (\ from\: :godot:`Object`, to\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_local_changed>`

Republishes the scene change of the participant this session decided is the local peer, bound when that row joins and released when the roster drops it. Another peer changing scene is not this session changing scene.

A participant raises its own ``scene_changed`` when the session takes or empties its seat while admitting or releasing it. A seat never moves on its own, so a game calls :ref:`scene_admit()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_admit>` and :ref:`scene_release()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_release>` and the seat follows.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_local_player_changed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**scene_local_player_changed**\ (\ player\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_local_player_changed>`

Emitted when the entity representing this peer changes, including to ``null`` when the route carrying it retires. Decided where a route goes live, because that is the only moment the result can change, and read off the entity's own record rather than through the wrapper. A session with no transport represents nobody, which an offline session returning unique id 1 would otherwise look like.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_spawned:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**scene_spawned**\ (\ scene\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_spawned>`

Emitted once for each scene the session takes in. It fires before any player has been admitted, so a listener reads the scene and not yet who is in it. A scene the session rejected to take in announces nothing.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_startup_spawned:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**scene_startup_spawned**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_startup_spawned>`

Emitted once server authority enters the session. It reports that startup finished rather than that anything spawned, so a session with nothing to spawn still announces and a listener waiting on it never waits forever.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_service_registered:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**service_registered**\ (\ service\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_service_registered>`

Emitted whenever a service is registered, whether through :ref:`service_register()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_service_register>` or through :ref:`Netw.service_register()<class_Netw_method_service_register>`. Registering the same instance again does nothing and announces nothing.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_service_unregistered:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**service_unregistered**\ (\ service\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_service_unregistered>`

Emitted by :ref:`service_unregister()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_service_unregister>`, and only when the type still names that service, so a service already replaced cannot unregister the one that replaced it.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_disconnect_requested:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_disconnect_requested**\ (\ peer_id\: :godot:`int`, reason\: :godot:`String`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_disconnect_requested>`

Emitted when a client asks to leave. Heard only by a session holding peer id 1, and ``peer_id`` is who actually sent the request rather than anything the request claims.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_ended:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_ended**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_ended>`

Emitted on leaving :ref:`SESSION_STATE_ONLINE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SESSION_STATE_ONLINE>`, so a session that failed before it ever connected never announces an ending.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_entered:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_entered**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_entered>`

Announced by the session machine on reaching :ref:`SESSION_STATE_ONLINE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SESSION_STATE_ONLINE>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_join_failed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_join_failed**\ (\ error\: :godot:`int`, reason\: :godot:`String`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_join_failed>`

This peer's own join was rejected and no player was seated for it. A game connects through :ref:`NetwConnectHandle.join_failed<class_NetwConnectHandle_signal_join_failed>` on :ref:`Netw.connection()<class_Netw_method_connection>` rather than here.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_join_submitted:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_join_submitted**\ (\ username\: :godot:`StringName`, args\: :godot:`Array`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_join_submitted>`

Emitted immediately before ``username`` and ``args`` go out, whether they travel to a server or are delivered to this session itself.

This announces that the join was sent and never that it was accepted. A server that rejects it announced this first.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_poll_started:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_poll_started**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_poll_started>`

Emitted at the start of each session poll. ``delta`` is the wall-clock gap since the previous one, and zero on the session's first.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_reclaimed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_reclaimed**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_reclaimed>`

Announced immediately after :ref:`session_ended<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_ended>`, so every listener of that signal has returned before this one fires. It is the phase, not a second ending.

A listener that frees nodes the session owns binds here rather than on :ref:`session_ended<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_ended>`, which is what keeps a listener of the announcement from being handed a node another listener already freed.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_server_disconnecting:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_server_disconnecting**\ (\ reason\: :godot:`String`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_server_disconnecting>`

Emitted when the server announces it is going away. A notice from anyone but the server is dropped.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_state_changed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_state_changed**\ (\ old_state\: :godot:`int`, new_state\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_state_changed>`

Announced by the session machine on every legal edge. An edge it rejects is not a transition, so it is not published.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_tree_paused:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_tree_paused**\ (\ reason\: :godot:`String`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_tree_paused>`

Emitted when server authority pauses the session. Pausing the engine is something a listener does in response rather than part of the decision, so the session announces this once and everything that reacts reads the same announcement. A notice from anyone but the server is dropped.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_tree_unpaused:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_tree_unpaused**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_tree_unpaused>`

The twin of :ref:`session_tree_paused<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_tree_paused>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_table_received:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**table_received**\ (\ table\: :godot:`RID`, tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_table_received>`

Emitted once for each table a commit produced or an arriving frame touched. ``tick`` is the one the commit stamped, so several commits inside one tick collapse to the last.

.. rst-class:: classref-section-separator

----

.. rst-class:: classref-descriptions-group

Enumerations
------------

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **Event**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SPAWNING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SPAWNING** = ``0``

An entity's record moved to its live stage outside a reparent and the wrapper is about to emit its own spawning signal. Belongs to the lifecycle group. detail is empty, and entity_id and peer both name the entity settling.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SPAWNED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SPAWNED** = ``1``

An entity's route finished binding and the entity is live. Belongs to the lifecycle group. detail is empty, and entity_id and peer both name the entity that just went live.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DESPAWNING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DESPAWNING** = ``2``

An entity's despawn began. Belongs to the lifecycle group. detail carries {reason}, and model carries {entity_id, peer_id}, which the terminal :ref:`EVENT_DESPAWNED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DESPAWNED>` row for the same route reuses since it carries none of its own.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DESPAWNED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DESPAWNED** = ``3``

An entity finished despawning and its route is retired. It is terminal, so its own entity_id is empty and its model is instead whatever the last non-empty model recorded for the route held, ordinarily :ref:`EVENT_DESPAWNING<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DESPAWNING>`'s {entity_id, peer_id}.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_REPARENTED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_REPARENTED** = ``4``

An entity was reparented. Belongs to the lifecycle group. detail carries {reason, moved}, where moved is true when the reparent also carried a target global position.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_STAGE_TRANSITION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_STAGE_TRANSITION** = ``5``

An entity's lifecycle stage changed. Belongs to the lifecycle group. detail carries {from, to}, the stage values on either side of the transition.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_CONTROL_CHANGED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_CONTROL_CHANGED** = ``6``

An entity's controlling peer changed. Belongs to the lifecycle group. detail carries {from, to}, and peer is the newly assigned controller.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_CONTROL_REQUESTED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_CONTROL_REQUESTED** = ``7``

A peer asked to take control of an entity. Belongs to the lifecycle group. detail carries {requester}, peer is the requester, and verdict is OK when authority granted control or an unauthorized error when it rejected.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_HIDDEN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_HIDDEN** = ``8``

An entity was hidden from its observers without being despawned. Belongs to the lifecycle group. detail is empty.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_INTEREST_ENTER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_INTEREST_ENTER** = ``16``

A peer began seeing an entity through one layer. Belongs to the interest group. detail carries {layer}, and peer is the one that began seeing it.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_INTEREST_EXIT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_INTEREST_EXIT** = ``17``

A peer stopped seeing an entity through one layer. Belongs to the interest group. detail carries {layer}, and peer is the one that stopped seeing it.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_OBSERVER_ENTERED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_OBSERVER_ENTERED** = ``18``

A peer began observing an entity through an awareness edge that is not a layer edge, and the entity's own observer_entered signal fires alongside it. Belongs to the interest group. detail carries {layer}, and peer is the observer.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_OBSERVER_LEFT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_OBSERVER_LEFT** = ``19``

A peer stopped observing an entity through an awareness edge that is not a layer edge, and the entity's own observer_left signal fires alongside it. Belongs to the interest group. detail carries {layer}, and peer is the observer.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_INTEREST_COMMIT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_INTEREST_COMMIT** = ``20``

A pending interest recompute was applied and observers were refreshed for the tick. Belongs to the interest group. detail is empty, and route is 0 because it reports the commit as a whole rather than one entity.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_EPISODE_OPEN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_EPISODE_OPEN** = ``32``

A prediction episode opened for an entity's slot. Belongs to the prediction group. detail carries {transition, attribution}, and model carries the full episode record the slot held at that moment.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_EPISODE_CLOSE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_EPISODE_CLOSE** = ``33``

A prediction episode closed for an entity's slot. Belongs to the prediction group. detail carries {transition, attribution} merged with the episode's disposition fields, and model carries the full episode record.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_EPISODE_FALLBACK:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_EPISODE_FALLBACK** = ``34``

A slot's prediction demoted into fallback after a breach it could not absorb. Belongs to the prediction group. detail carries {transition, attribution, demoted}, and model carries the full episode record.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PREDICT_DRIVE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_PREDICT_DRIVE** = ``35``

A slot drove a new predicted transition. Belongs to the prediction group. detail carries {transition, label, kind, fresh}, and the row is only raised when the drive actually produced a transition.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PREDICT_CONSUME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_PREDICT_CONSUME** = ``36``

A slot consumed a buffered predicted input. Belongs to the prediction group. detail carries {depth, buffer, action}.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PREDICT_EVALUATE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_PREDICT_EVALUATE** = ``37``

A slot's prediction was judged against the authoritative state it received. Belongs to the prediction group. detail carries {transition, divergence, corrected}.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PREDICT_RECOVER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_PREDICT_RECOVER** = ``38``

A slot applied a recovery plan after a divergence. Belongs to the prediction group. detail carries {transition, skip, teleport}.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DIVERGENCE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DIVERGENCE** = ``39``

A slot's prediction diverged from the authoritative state by more than its tolerance, and the handle's own divergence_detected signal fires alongside it. Belongs to the prediction group. detail carries {transition, attribution, divergence}.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_RECOVERY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_RECOVERY** = ``40``

A slot corrected its predicted state after a divergence, and the handle's own recovered signal fires alongside it. Belongs to the prediction group. detail carries {transition, attribution, teleport, moved}, where moved is the dictionary of properties the correction actually wrote.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_GATE_SYNC:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_GATE_SYNC** = ``48``

An inbound sync frame, on the sync, row, row-delta, row-window or delta channel, passed or failed admission. Belongs to the gates group. detail is empty, and verdict is what the session returned.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_GATE_SPAWN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_GATE_SPAWN** = ``49``

An inbound spawn frame passed or failed admission. Belongs to the gates group. detail is empty, route is 0 since spawn admission is not per-entity, and verdict is what the session returned.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_GATE_TABLE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_GATE_TABLE** = ``50``

An inbound table frame passed or failed admission. Belongs to the gates group. detail is empty, route is 0, and verdict is what the session returned.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_GATE_PREDICT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_GATE_PREDICT** = ``51``

An inbound prediction frame, command, ack, relay or relay request, passed or failed admission. Belongs to the gates group. detail is empty, and verdict is what the session returned.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SYNC_ENCODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SYNC_ENCODE** = ``64``

A sync frame finished encoding for a peer. Belongs to the stages group. detail carries {bytes}, the encoded size, and peer is the recipient.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SYNC_DECODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SYNC_DECODE** = ``65``

A sync frame finished decoding for an entity's component. Belongs to the stages group. detail carries {comp}, and verdict is what decoding returned.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_GATHER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_GATHER** = ``66``

A deferred send pass gathered row offers into outbound sends. Belongs to the stages group. detail carries {offers, sends}, the offers considered and the sends actually produced.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_APPLY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_APPLY** = ``67``

A batch of received frames finished applying. Belongs to the stages group. detail carries {frames}, the frame count, peer is the sender, and verdict is the worst error the batch produced, or OK.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SPAWN_DECLARE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SPAWN_DECLARE** = ``68``

An entity was declared for spawning. Belongs to the stages group. detail is empty, route is the declared entity's route, and verdict is what the session returned.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SPAWN_RECONCILE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SPAWN_RECONCILE** = ``69``

The spawn planner reconciled the roster against connected peers. Belongs to the stages group. detail is empty, route is 0, and verdict is an unconfigured error when the planner produced no plan, or OK.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SPAWN_CONSTRUCT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SPAWN_CONSTRUCT** = ``70``

The spawn pipeline finished constructing an entity's node. Belongs to the stages group. detail carries {built}, whether construction actually produced a node.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DISPLAY_RECORD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DISPLAY_RECORD** = ``71``

A display track recorded a value for a tick. Belongs to the stages group. detail carries {track, tick}.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DISPLAY_PUMP:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DISPLAY_PUMP** = ``72``

A display runtime pumped its interpolation. Belongs to the stages group. detail carries {tick}, the display tick it pumped to.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DISPLAY_WRITE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DISPLAY_WRITE** = ``73``

A display track wrote a value onto an entity. Belongs to the stages group. detail carries {track}, and verdict is what :ref:`_display_write()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__display_write>` returned.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_TABLE_COMMIT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_TABLE_COMMIT** = ``74``

A table finished committing its dirty rows for a tick. Belongs to the stages group. detail carries {stage, rows}, where stage is always "table" and rows is the committed row count, and verdict is what the commit returned.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_VERDICT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_VERDICT** = ``96``

A rejection was counted toward what :ref:`stats_get_verdict_count()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_stats_get_verdict_count>` returns. Belongs to the verdicts group. detail carries {stage, counted}, where stage is always "gate" and counted says whether the session actually recorded it, and verdict is the error being counted.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SEAM_MISUSE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SEAM_MISUSE** = ``97``

An override point a game replaced returned something the session cannot use, so the default result stood. Belongs to the verdicts group. detail carries {seam, stage, reason}, and verdict is always an invalid-data error.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SESSION_STATE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SESSION_STATE** = ``112``

Belongs to the session group and names a session state transition. Nothing in the session emits it yet, so no detail shape is established for it, and a watcher should not assume one.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PEER_JOINED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_PEER_JOINED** = ``113``

A peer connected to the session. Belongs to the session group. detail carries {peer}, route is 0, and peer names the peer that connected.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PEER_LEFT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_PEER_LEFT** = ``114``

A peer disconnected from the session. Belongs to the session group. detail carries {peer}, route is 0, and peer names the peer that disconnected.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PEER_AUTH_FAILED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_PEER_AUTH_FAILED** = ``115``

A peer failed authentication. Belongs to the session group. detail carries {peer}, and peer names the peer that was rejected.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SCENE_LIVE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SCENE_LIVE** = ``116``

A scene container went live for a route. Belongs to the session group. detail carries {scene}, the scene's name.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DATAGRAM_SENT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DATAGRAM_SENT** = ``128``

A carrier datagram was sent to a peer. Belongs to the carrier group. detail carries {size, seq, reliable}, and peer is the recipient.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DATAGRAM_RECEIVED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DATAGRAM_RECEIVED** = ``129``

A carrier datagram was received from a peer. Belongs to the carrier group. detail carries {size, seq, reliable}, where seq is -1 for a reliable datagram, and peer is the sender.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DATAGRAM_MALFORMED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DATAGRAM_MALFORMED** = ``130``

A carrier datagram from a peer failed to parse as a known frame. Belongs to the carrier group. detail carries {size}, peer is the sender, and verdict is always an invalid-data error.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_ACK_ADVANCED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_ACK_ADVANCED** = ``131``

A peer's acknowledged sequence advanced. Belongs to the carrier group. detail carries {ack}, the newly acknowledged sequence, and peer is the peer whose ack moved.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_Phase:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **Phase**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Phase>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PHASE_BEFORE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Phase<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Phase>` **EVENT_PHASE_BEFORE** = ``0``

The row was recorded before the act it names ran, so what it reports is an intention rather than an outcome and no verdict on it is settled yet. Only an act the session announces twice emits one.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PHASE_AFTER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Phase<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Phase>` **EVENT_PHASE_AFTER** = ``1``

The row was recorded once the act it names had run, which is what an event row is unless it says otherwise. A sink counting acts counts this phase and ignores :ref:`EVENT_PHASE_BEFORE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PHASE_BEFORE>`, or it counts the announced ones twice.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_TableParam:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **TableParam**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TableParam>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TABLE_PARAM_RELIABLE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TableParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TableParam>` **TABLE_PARAM_RELIABLE** = ``0``

:godot:`bool`. Whether commits ride the reliable lane, passed to :ref:`table_set_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_set_param>`. A table that changes rarely wants this, because it has no next commit to heal a lost datagram with, and a table published every tick does not. Table params are local configuration rather than wire surface, so one never enters :ref:`table_get_wire_hash()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_get_wire_hash>` and a later value is not a wire event.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneMove:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **SceneMove**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneMove>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_MOVE_REFUSED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneMove<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneMove>` **SCENE_MOVE_REFUSED** = ``0``

The move is rejected because its source or destination is not live.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_MOVE_ALREADY_THERE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneMove<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneMove>` **SCENE_MOVE_ALREADY_THERE** = ``1``

The mover already belongs to the destination.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_RecordKind:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **RecordKind**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_RecordKind>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_RECORD_KIND_STATE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`RecordKind<enum_NetwMultiplayer_RecordKind>` **RECORD_KIND_STATE** = ``1``

The server's own truth, authored by it and kept in a rewind timeline. A body pose is the usual example.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_RECORD_KIND_INPUT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`RecordKind<enum_NetwMultiplayer_RecordKind>` **RECORD_KIND_INPUT** = ``2``

A client's claim, sent to the server only and verified by it, also kept for rewind. Movement keys are the usual example.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_RECORD_KIND_BROADCAST:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`RecordKind<enum_NetwMultiplayer_RecordKind>` **RECORD_KIND_BROADCAST** = ``3``

Trusted display sent to everyone, checked by nobody and kept nowhere. An aim arrow is the usual example.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_EntityState:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **EntityState**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EntityState>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_UNKNOWN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EntityState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EntityState>` **ENTITY_STATE_UNKNOWN** = ``0``

The route was never issued, or was issued by a peer this one has not heard from.

\ :ref:`EntityState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EntityState>` is what this peer knows about a route, and nothing else knows it for them. The four values are ordered and a route only moves forward through them, so a route that is dead never comes back to life. Whether this peer can see the entity is a separate question, and a route may reach :ref:`ENTITY_STATE_ABSENT<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_ABSENT>` and return without spending a life.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_LIVE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EntityState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EntityState>` **ENTITY_STATE_LIVE** = ``1``

The entity exists and accepts traffic.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_LINGERING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EntityState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EntityState>` **ENTITY_STATE_LINGERING** = ``2``

Despawn has begun. Frames still in flight are dropped rather than treated as addressing nothing.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_DEAD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EntityState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EntityState>` **ENTITY_STATE_DEAD** = ``3``

The route is retired and will not be reissued. Losing sight of an entity is not this, and reads :ref:`ENTITY_STATE_ABSENT<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_ABSENT>` instead.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_ABSENT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EntityState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EntityState>` **ENTITY_STATE_ABSENT** = ``4``

This peer holds no copy of the route right now and could be shown one again. Only a receiving peer reaches this, because the server never loses sight of its own entities.

It ranks alongside :ref:`ENTITY_STATE_LIVE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_LIVE>` rather than past it. The route keeps naming the same entity, returning costs nothing, and a real despawn still retires it. See :ref:`entity_hidden<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_hidden>` and :ref:`LEAVE_POLICY_HIDE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LEAVE_POLICY_HIDE>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_LayerPolicy:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **LayerPolicy**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LayerPolicy>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LAYER_POLICY_HIDE_FROM_OUTSIDERS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`LayerPolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LayerPolicy>` **LAYER_POLICY_HIDE_FROM_OUTSIDERS** = ``0``

Only members of the layer see its entities. The usual choice for a team or a private room.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LAYER_POLICY_HIDE_FROM_INSIDERS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`LayerPolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LayerPolicy>` **LAYER_POLICY_HIDE_FROM_INSIDERS** = ``1``

Only non-members see its entities. The usual choice for something a player should not see their own copy of.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_EmbedPhase:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **EmbedPhase**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EmbedPhase>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EMBED_PHASE_DECLARING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EmbedPhase<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EmbedPhase>` **EMBED_PHASE_DECLARING** = ``0``

Scripts declare through the ``Netw.configure_*`` family, and a stock :godot:`MultiplayerSpawner` or :godot:`MultiplayerSynchronizer` arrives through :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.object_configuration_add() <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_method_object_configuration_add>`. Nothing acts on either yet, and authoring is only in contract here. A :ref:`NetwSessionConfig<class_NetwSessionConfig>`, :ref:`NetwClockConfig<class_NetwClockConfig>` or :ref:`NetwLagCompensationConfig<class_NetwLagCompensationConfig>` pushed through that door is rejected in every phase, because those three are declared once and consumed once.

\ :ref:`EmbedPhase<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EmbedPhase>` says how far a session has come in starting up, and :ref:`state<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_state>` says whether it is connected. The two are separate questions and a session returns both at once.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EMBED_PHASE_SETTLING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EmbedPhase<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EmbedPhase>` **EMBED_PHASE_SETTLING** = ``1``

The embedding signalled the authored world is loaded, and the session resolves the winning declarations as one ordered step.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EMBED_PHASE_LIVE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EmbedPhase<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EmbedPhase>` **EMBED_PHASE_LIVE** = ``2``

Every declaration has resolved and the session may now host or join. A session reaches here through :ref:`embed_settle()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_settle>` and never leaves.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_LeavePolicy:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **LeavePolicy**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LeavePolicy>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LEAVE_POLICY_HIDE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`LeavePolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LeavePolicy>` **LEAVE_POLICY_HIDE** = ``0``

Tell the peer it no longer holds a copy. The copy is freed, the route reads :ref:`ENTITY_STATE_ABSENT<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_ABSENT>` on that peer, and a later admission binds the same identity again. This is not a despawn, because an entity a peer stops seeing has not stopped existing.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LEAVE_POLICY_RETAIN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`LeavePolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LeavePolicy>` **LEAVE_POLICY_RETAIN** = ``1``

Keep the copy and stop updating it. It resumes from a stale pose when interest returns, with no spawn cost.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LEAVE_POLICY_CUSTOM:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`LeavePolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LeavePolicy>` **LEAVE_POLICY_CUSTOM** = ``2``

Neither. The game decides, through the layer's own handler.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_PerceptionPolicy:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **PerceptionPolicy**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PerceptionPolicy>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PERCEPTION_POLICY_HIDE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PerceptionPolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PerceptionPolicy>` **PERCEPTION_POLICY_HIDE** = ``0``

Hide the copy that was kept, so it stops being drawn where it was last seen. :ref:`LeavePolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LeavePolicy>` decides whether the copy is kept at all, and :ref:`PerceptionPolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PerceptionPolicy>` decides whether a kept copy is drawn.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PERCEPTION_POLICY_SHOW:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PerceptionPolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PerceptionPolicy>` **PERCEPTION_POLICY_SHOW** = ``1``

Keep drawing the retained copy at its last known pose. A :ref:`LEAVE_POLICY_RETAIN<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LEAVE_POLICY_RETAIN>` entity is still present, so something must say whether it is drawn.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PERCEPTION_POLICY_CUSTOM:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PerceptionPolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PerceptionPolicy>` **PERCEPTION_POLICY_CUSTOM** = ``2``

Neither. The game decides, through the layer's own handler.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **DisplayRole**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTO:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>` **DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTO** = ``0``

Resolve from :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>` control and :godot:`Node.multiplayer_authority <Node#class_Node_property_multiplayer_authority>`. The default, and correct for almost every entity.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_REMOTE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>` **DISPLAY_ROLE_REMOTE** = ``1``

Interpolate replicated snapshots. What a peer uses for somebody else's entity.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_PREDICTED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>` **DISPLAY_ROLE_PREDICTED** = ``2``

Follow the locally predicted body. What a player uses for their own entity.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_DISABLED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>` **DISPLAY_ROLE_DISABLED** = ``3``

No smoothing. The node is left alone for the game to drive.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTHORITY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>` **DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTHORITY** = ``4``

Sample the locally authored simulation each tick and play it back, so the server sees its own world smoothed the same way.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **DisplayPump**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PUMP_UNRESOLVED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayPump<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>` **DISPLAY_PUMP_UNRESOLVED** = ``-1``

No pump has been chosen yet. A runtime starts here and resolves to one of the other values the first time its role is settled.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PUMP_DISABLED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayPump<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>` **DISPLAY_PUMP_DISABLED** = ``0``

Nothing is pumped. Matches :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_DISABLED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_DISABLED>` and the unresolved :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTO<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTO>` case, where the node is left for the game to drive.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PUMP_REMOTE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayPump<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>` **DISPLAY_PUMP_REMOTE** = ``1``

Sample the recorded history along the session's display timeline, :ref:`TIMELINE_MODE_BUFFERED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TIMELINE_MODE_BUFFERED>` or :ref:`TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST>`. What :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_REMOTE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_REMOTE>` pumps.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PUMP_BRACKETED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayPump<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>` **DISPLAY_PUMP_BRACKETED** = ``2``

Sample the recorded history bracketed between the previous and current tick, one tick behind. What :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTHORITY<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTHORITY>` always pumps, and what :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_PREDICTED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_PREDICTED>` pumps under :ref:`PREDICTED_MODE_BRACKETED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICTED_MODE_BRACKETED>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PUMP_CHASE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayPump<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>` **DISPLAY_PUMP_CHASE** = ``3``

Ease the visual toward the live predicted body every frame instead of sampling history. What :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_PREDICTED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_PREDICTED>` pumps under :ref:`PREDICTED_MODE_CHASE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICTED_MODE_CHASE>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictedMode:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **PredictedMode**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictedMode>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICTED_MODE_CHASE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictedMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictedMode>` **PREDICTED_MODE_CHASE** = ``0``

Ease the visual toward the live predicted body every frame. Most responsive, and shows correction ripple.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICTED_MODE_BRACKETED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictedMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictedMode>` **PREDICTED_MODE_BRACKETED** = ``1``

Interpolate between the previous and current tick samples. Steadier, and one tick behind.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_TimelineMode:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **TimelineMode**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TimelineMode>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TIMELINE_MODE_BUFFERED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TimelineMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TimelineMode>` **TIMELINE_MODE_BUFFERED** = ``0``

Render behind the newest sample by the jitter buffer, always a delayed truth. The default, and the only mode the server ever resolves.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TimelineMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TimelineMode>` **TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST** = ``1``

Project along the last replicated velocity, trading buffer delay for extrapolation error. Collision blind, so a body projected toward a wall penetrates it until the bounce sample arrives. Prefer :ref:`TIMELINE_MODE_BUFFERED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TIMELINE_MODE_BUFFERED>` for anything that meets world geometry.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_SyncMode:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **SyncMode**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SyncMode>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SYNC_MODE_SNAP:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SyncMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SyncMode>` **SYNC_MODE_SNAP** = ``0``

Jump the local tick straight to the measured target on every sample. The local tick is always driven back toward the server's, and this only decides whether that happens in one step or is spread across frames.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SYNC_MODE_STRETCH:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SyncMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SyncMode>` **SYNC_MODE_STRETCH** = ``1``

Nudge the tick accumulator toward the target, snapping only once the divergence exceeds the configured panic threshold.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_MismatchAction:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **MismatchAction**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_MismatchAction>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_MISMATCH_ACTION_WARN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`MismatchAction<enum_NetwMultiplayer_MismatchAction>` **MISMATCH_ACTION_WARN** = ``0``

Log the tickrate difference and carry on. The default, because a tickrate difference is a configuration mistake rather than a protocol violation.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_MISMATCH_ACTION_DISCONNECT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`MismatchAction<enum_NetwMultiplayer_MismatchAction>` **MISMATCH_ACTION_DISCONNECT** = ``1``

Close the peer. Choose this only when a mismatched peer cannot be allowed to simulate at all, because it gives the game no way to say what happened.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_MISMATCH_ACTION_SIGNAL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`MismatchAction<enum_NetwMultiplayer_MismatchAction>` **MISMATCH_ACTION_SIGNAL** = ``2``

Emit :ref:`clock_tickrate_mismatch<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_tickrate_mismatch>` and leave the decision to the game. Choose this when the game wants to show the mismatch before deciding.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_LayerParam:

enum **LayerParam**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LayerParam>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LAYER_PARAM_POLICY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`LayerParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LayerParam>` **LAYER_PARAM_POLICY** = ``0``

The layer's :ref:`LayerPolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LayerPolicy>`, which composes its viewer set. Layer params are passed to :ref:`interest_layer_set_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_layer_set_param>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LAYER_PARAM_LEAVE_POLICY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`LayerParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LayerParam>` **LAYER_PARAM_LEAVE_POLICY** = ``1``

The layer's :ref:`LeavePolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LeavePolicy>`, which decides what happens on the wire when the layer stops admitting an entity.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LAYER_PARAM_PERCEPTION_POLICY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`LayerParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LayerParam>` **LAYER_PARAM_PERCEPTION_POLICY** = ``2``

The layer's :ref:`PerceptionPolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PerceptionPolicy>`, which decides what the local player sees when the layer stops admitting an entity.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **TransportParam**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_PARAM_PEER_CLASS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam>` **TRANSPORT_PARAM_PEER_CLASS** = ``0``

:godot:`StringName` class of the peer this transport builds, which is the name :ref:`transport_find()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_find>` takes. Transport params are read through :ref:`transport_get_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_get_param>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_PARAM_DISPLAY_NAME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam>` **TRANSPORT_PARAM_DISPLAY_NAME** = ``1``

:godot:`String` to call this transport in a picker.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_PARAM_ADDRESS_LABEL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam>` **TRANSPORT_PARAM_ADDRESS_LABEL** = ``2``

:godot:`String` to title the address field with, such as an IP for :godot:`ENetMultiplayerPeer`, a URL for a WebSocket, or a room id for WebRTC.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_PARAM_ADDRESS_PLACEHOLDER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam>` **TRANSPORT_PARAM_ADDRESS_PLACEHOLDER** = ``3``

:godot:`String` to show in the empty address field.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_PARAM_ADDRESS_HELP:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam>` **TRANSPORT_PARAM_ADDRESS_HELP** = ``4``

:godot:`String` sentence under the address field, empty when it needs none.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_PARAM_CAPABILITIES:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam>` **TRANSPORT_PARAM_CAPABILITIES** = ``5``

:godot:`int` mask of :ref:`TransportCapability<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportCapability>`, saying what this transport can do on this build and platform.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_PARAM_HOST_SETTINGS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam>` **TRANSPORT_PARAM_HOST_SETTINGS** = ``6``

:godot:`Dictionary` describing the fields a host form asks for, one entry per field, typed by its own value. It is what :ref:`transport_create_peer()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_create_peer>` reads back, and a game that builds its own peer never sees it.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_PARAM_CLIENT_SETTINGS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam>` **TRANSPORT_PARAM_CLIENT_SETTINGS** = ``7``

:godot:`Dictionary` describing the join form fields. It has the same shape as :ref:`TRANSPORT_PARAM_HOST_SETTINGS<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_PARAM_HOST_SETTINGS>`. Shared fields, such as an ENet ``port``, appear in both dictionaries.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportCapability:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **TransportCapability**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportCapability>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_AVAILABLE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportCapability<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportCapability>` **TRANSPORT_AVAILABLE** = ``1``

The transport reports itself usable on this build. A picker greys out a row that does not.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_CAN_HOST:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportCapability<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportCapability>` **TRANSPORT_CAN_HOST** = ``2``

This build can listen over this transport at all, which is how a web build stops offering to host.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_CAN_PROBE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportCapability<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportCapability>` **TRANSPORT_CAN_PROBE** = ``4``

A row of this transport can be asked what is there without joining it, which is what :ref:`endpoint_probe()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_probe>` needs.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_CAN_BROWSE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportCapability<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportCapability>` **TRANSPORT_CAN_BROWSE** = ``8``

The transport lists what it can reach, which is what :ref:`endpoint_refresh()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_refresh>` asks of it.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_ACCEPTS_EMPTY_ADDRESS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TransportCapability<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportCapability>` **TRANSPORT_ACCEPTS_EMPTY_ADDRESS** = ``16``

An empty address means something to this transport, which lets an Add form accept a blank field for a transport that finds its own host.

The transport that builds peers for this row, as a :godot:`RID` handle. Read only, because a row is the pairing of a transport with an address and changing either makes a different row.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_PARAM_ADDRESS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EndpointParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointParam>` **ENDPOINT_PARAM_ADDRESS** = ``1``

The address this row reaches, as the transport spells it. Read only, for the reason above.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_PARAM_DISPLAY_NAME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EndpointParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointParam>` **ENDPOINT_PARAM_DISPLAY_NAME** = ``2``

What a server browser draws for this row. The only field :ref:`endpoint_set_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_set_param>` will write.

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointState:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **EndpointState**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointState>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_STATE_FLAGS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EndpointState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointState>` **ENDPOINT_STATE_FLAGS** = ``0``

The row's flags, as a bitmask of :ref:`ENDPOINT_FLAG_CALLER<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_FLAG_CALLER>`, :ref:`ENDPOINT_FLAG_AVAILABLE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_FLAG_AVAILABLE>` and :ref:`ENDPOINT_FLAG_OBSERVED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_FLAG_OBSERVED>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_STATE_STATUS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EndpointState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointState>` **ENDPOINT_STATE_STATUS** = ``1``

What the last probe returned, as an :godot:`@GlobalScope.Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>`. It reads :godot:`@GlobalScope.FAILED <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_FAILED>` before anything has probed the row, which is not the same as a probe that failed.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_STATE_INFO:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EndpointState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointState>` **ENDPOINT_STATE_INFO** = ``2``

The :ref:`NetwServerInfo<class_NetwServerInfo>` the last probe reply carried, or ``null`` when nothing has returned.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator
