.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointFlags:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **EndpointFlags**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointFlags>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_FLAG_CALLER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EndpointFlags<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointFlags>` **ENDPOINT_FLAG_CALLER** = ``1``

The row was added by a caller through :ref:`endpoint_add()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_add>` rather than found by a listing.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_FLAG_AVAILABLE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EndpointFlags<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointFlags>` **ENDPOINT_FLAG_AVAILABLE** = ``2``

The last probe reached a server that returned.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_FLAG_OBSERVED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`EndpointFlags<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EndpointFlags>` **ENDPOINT_FLAG_OBSERVED** = ``4``

The row was seen in a listing rather than named by a caller. A row can carry this and :ref:`ENDPOINT_FLAG_CALLER<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_FLAG_CALLER>` at once.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneParam:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **SceneParam**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneParam>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_PARAM_LABEL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneParam>` **SCENE_PARAM_LABEL** = ``0``

:godot:`StringName` naming what kind of scene this is. Two live copies of one arena share the name, because a scene is identified by its :godot:`RID`. See :ref:`NetwEntity.scene_label<class_NetwEntity_property_scene_label>`. Every :ref:`SceneParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneParam>` is read and written through :ref:`scene_get_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_get_param>` and :ref:`scene_set_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_set_param>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_PARAM_ISOLATION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneParam>` **SCENE_PARAM_ISOLATION** = ``1``

The scene's :ref:`SceneIsolation<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneIsolation>`. Write-once before the entity arms, because it chooses the container every peer builds.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_PARAM_PROCESSING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneParam>` **SCENE_PARAM_PROCESSING** = ``2``

:godot:`bool` gating whether the scene's subtree processes. Freeze and resume.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneEvent:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **SceneEvent**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneEvent>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_EVENT_PARTICIPANT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneEvent<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneEvent>` **SCENE_EVENT_PARTICIPANT** = ``0``

A participant was admitted or released, and ``subject`` is its peer id. Every :ref:`scene_observe()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_observe>` callback takes ``(present: bool, subject: Variant)``, so one registration hears both directions and a caller that only wants arrivals ignores the rest.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_EVENT_PLAYER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneEvent<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneEvent>` **SCENE_EVENT_PLAYER** = ``1``

A player entity entered or left, and ``subject`` is its entity RID.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_EVENT_ENTITY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneEvent<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneEvent>` **SCENE_EVENT_ENTITY** = ``2``

Any tracked entity entered or left, and ``subject`` is its entity RID. Players report here too, so a caller wanting only them reads :ref:`SCENE_EVENT_PLAYER<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_EVENT_PLAYER>` instead.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneChange:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **SceneChange**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneChange>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_CHANGE_SESSION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneChange<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneChange>` **SCENE_CHANGE_SESSION** = ``0``

Every participant converges on the destination and every other live scene retires. Isolation, the number of live scenes, and whether authority also holds a local player do not change what this means.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_CHANGE_PARTICIPANT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneChange<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneChange>` **SCENE_CHANGE_PARTICIPANT** = ``1``

One participant travels with the player entities it is enrolled in, and every world keeps running. A change at this scope with no participant to resolve is rejected rather than widened to the session.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_CHANGE_SCENE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneChange<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneChange>` **SCENE_CHANGE_SCENE** = ``2``

The occupants of the caller's own world travel and that world retires, leaving other worlds untouched. A change at this scope with no source world to resolve is rejected.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneIsolation:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **SceneIsolation**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneIsolation>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_ISOLATION_NONE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneIsolation<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneIsolation>` **SCENE_ISOLATION_NONE** = ``0``

The scene shares the session's world and its root is a plain :godot:`Node`. Each scene chooses its own isolation, so one session can hold a shared lobby and several isolated match worlds at once.

It is decided before the scene spawns. Every peer builds the root from the same recipe, and a peer that built the wrong kind has nothing to hang the scene's children on.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_ISOLATION_OWN_WORLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`SceneIsolation<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneIsolation>` **SCENE_ISOLATION_OWN_WORLD** = ``1``

A hosting peer builds the scene an offscreen :godot:`SubViewport` with its own world, so two live scenes never share a physics space. A peer that only views the scene still builds a plain :godot:`Node`, because only the host simulates.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **ClockParam**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE** = ``0``

How many simulation ticks run per second, as an :godot:`int`. The one clock value that must be identical on every peer, so :ref:`clock_set_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_param>` rejects it with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNAUTHORIZED <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNAUTHORIZED>` and a declared :ref:`NetwClockConfig<class_NetwClockConfig>` is its only door.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_SYNC_MODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_SYNC_MODE** = ``1``

Which correction shape calibration takes, as one of :ref:`SyncMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SyncMode>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_PING_INTERVAL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_PING_INTERVAL** = ``2``

Seconds between the pings that refresh the round-trip estimate, as a :godot:`float`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME** = ``3``

The most ticks one frame may run, as an :godot:`int`. It is a ceiling rather than a target, and the time a capped frame could not spend is kept for the next one.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_STALL_THRESHOLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_STALL_THRESHOLD** = ``4``

The frame length above which a frame is read as a hitch rather than as simulated time, in seconds, as a :godot:`float`. Crossing it resets the accumulator.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_PANIC_SNAP_THRESHOLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_PANIC_SNAP_THRESHOLD** = ``5``

The divergence in ticks above which :ref:`SYNC_MODE_STRETCH<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SYNC_MODE_STRETCH>` gives up and snaps, as an :godot:`int`. Past it the gap is a desync rather than drift.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_STRETCH_NUDGE_FACTOR:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_STRETCH_NUDGE_FACTOR** = ``6``

The fraction of the remaining divergence :ref:`SYNC_MODE_STRETCH<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SYNC_MODE_STRETCH>` closes each frame, as a :godot:`float`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET** = ``7``

How many ticks the display trails the simulation, as an :godot:`int`. The buffer the smoothing spends.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_LEAD_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_LEAD_TICKS** = ``8``

The explicit margin, in ticks, the calibration target is placed ahead of the server's own position, as a :godot:`float`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_MULTIPLIER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_MULTIPLIER** = ``9``

How strongly measured jitter widens :ref:`CLOCK_MONITOR_RECOMMENDED_DISPLAY_OFFSET<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_RECOMMENDED_DISPLAY_OFFSET>`, as a :godot:`float`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_WINDOW:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_WINDOW** = ``10``

How many recent round-trip samples the average and the jitter are taken over, as an :godot:`int`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_STABILITY_THRESHOLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_STABILITY_THRESHOLD** = ``11``

The jitter below which the link counts as stable, in seconds, as a :godot:`float`. What :ref:`clock_is_stable()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_stable>` compares against.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_USE_PHYSICS_INTERPOLATION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_USE_PHYSICS_INTERPOLATION** = ``12``

Whether the tick factor tracks the physics interpolation fraction, as a :godot:`bool`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_ENABLE_DRIFT_LOGGING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_ENABLE_DRIFT_LOGGING** = ``13``

Whether average drift is logged over rolling windows, as a :godot:`bool`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_MANUAL_TICK:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_MANUAL_TICK** = ``14``

Whether the schedule ignores frame pumps so a rig can own it, as a :godot:`bool`. What a deterministic stepper holds while it drives.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICK_FACTOR_OVERRIDE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_TICK_FACTOR_OVERRIDE** = ``15``

A fixed value for :ref:`CLOCK_MONITOR_TICK_FACTOR<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_TICK_FACTOR>` in place of the measured one, as a :godot:`float`. Negative restores the measurement.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **ClockMonitor**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT** = ``0``

The most recent round-trip time, in seconds.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT_AVG:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT_AVG** = ``1``

The round-trip time averaged over :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_WINDOW<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_WINDOW>` samples, in seconds.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT_JITTER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT_JITTER** = ``2``

The spread of those samples, in seconds.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_ONE_WAY_LATENCY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_ONE_WAY_LATENCY** = ``3``

Half of :ref:`CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT_AVG<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT_AVG>`, in seconds.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_TICKTIME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_TICKTIME** = ``4``

One tick's worth of simulated time, in seconds, which is one divided by :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_TICK_FACTOR:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_TICK_FACTOR** = ``5``

How far the current frame has advanced between the last tick and the next, from zero to one.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_TICK_PHASE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_TICK_PHASE** = ``6``

Where the clock sits inside the current tick, from zero to one. What a pong carries so a calibration target can be a continuous position rather than a whole tick.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_TICK_ACCUMULATOR:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_TICK_ACCUMULATOR** = ``7``

The frame time banked toward the next tick, in seconds.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_PHYSICS_FACTOR:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_PHYSICS_FACTOR** = ``8``

Physics frames per tick, unrounded. :ref:`clock_get_physics_steps_per_tick()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_physics_steps_per_tick>` is this as a whole number.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_RECOMMENDED_DISPLAY_OFFSET:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_RECOMMENDED_DISPLAY_OFFSET** = ``9``

The display offset the measured jitter suggests, in ticks. It is a recommendation, and :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET>` is what the clock actually uses.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_PHYSICS_FRAMES:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_PHYSICS_FRAMES** = ``10``

How many physics frames this process has run since the clock started counting.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_POLLS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_POLLS** = ``11``

How many session polls it has run over the same span.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_WALL_SECONDS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_WALL_SECONDS** = ``12``

The wall-clock seconds that span covers.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_PHYSICS_HZ:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_PHYSICS_HZ** = ``13``

:ref:`CLOCK_MONITOR_PHYSICS_FRAMES<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_PHYSICS_FRAMES>` over :ref:`CLOCK_MONITOR_WALL_SECONDS<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_WALL_SECONDS>`. What the engine actually delivered, which is how a starved host is told apart from a mistuned one.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_POLL_HZ:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_POLL_HZ** = ``14``

:ref:`CLOCK_MONITOR_POLLS<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_POLLS>` over :ref:`CLOCK_MONITOR_WALL_SECONDS<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_WALL_SECONDS>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **DisplayParam**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_ROLE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_ROLE** = ``0``

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>`. How the entity is drawn. This is the only one most games ever set, and it is usually left at :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTO<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTO>`.

Every :ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` is read and written through :ref:`display_get_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_display_get_param>` and :ref:`display_set_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_display_set_param>`, and the note after each name is the :godot:`Variant` type it takes. :ref:`NetwDisplayHandle<class_NetwDisplayHandle>` publishes twelve of them as named members. :ref:`DISPLAY_PARAM_MAX_FORECAST_TICKS<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_MAX_FORECAST_TICKS>` and :ref:`DISPLAY_PARAM_CHASE_GLIDE_TIME<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_CHASE_GLIDE_TIME>` are reached through these two methods only.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_PREDICTED_MODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_PREDICTED_MODE** = ``1``

:ref:`PredictedMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictedMode>`. How a predicted entity turns its body into a pose.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_PREDICTED_SMOOTH_TIME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_PREDICTED_SMOOTH_TIME** = ``2``

:godot:`float` seconds. How long a predicted visual takes to absorb a correction.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_CHASE_GLIDE_TIME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_CHASE_GLIDE_TIME** = ``3``

:godot:`float` seconds. How long :ref:`PREDICTED_MODE_CHASE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICTED_MODE_CHASE>` takes to close the gap to the live body.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_TIMELINE_MODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_TIMELINE_MODE** = ``4``

:ref:`TimelineMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TimelineMode>`. Where on the timeline a remote entity renders.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_MAX_FORECAST_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_MAX_FORECAST_TICKS** = ``5``

:godot:`int` ticks. How far :ref:`TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST>` may project past the newest sample before it gives up and holds.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_SMART_DILATION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_SMART_DILATION** = ``6``

:godot:`bool`. Whether the buffer may stretch playback to ride out jitter instead of starving.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_MAX_EXTRA_DILATION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_MAX_EXTRA_DILATION** = ``7``

:godot:`float` as a fraction of normal speed. The ceiling on that stretch.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_LAG_ADAPT_RATE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_LAG_ADAPT_RATE** = ``8``

:godot:`float`. How quickly the buffer re-targets when the measured lag moves. Higher follows the network faster and jitters more.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_STARVATION_GROWTH:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_STARVATION_GROWTH** = ``9``

:godot:`float`. How much the buffer grows after a starvation, buying margin at the cost of latency.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_FLOOR_SMOOTHING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_FLOOR_SMOOTHING** = ``10``

:godot:`float`. Smoothing applied to the buffer floor so it settles rather than chases every sample.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_STARVATION_GRACE_FRAMES:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_STARVATION_GRACE_FRAMES** = ``11``

:godot:`int` frames. How many starved frames pass before the runtime treats the stream as genuinely stalled.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_TRACE_INTERVAL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_TRACE_INTERVAL** = ``12``

:godot:`int` ticks. How often display tracing samples, or ``0`` to trace nothing.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_VISUAL_ROOT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_VISUAL_ROOT** = ``13``

:godot:`int` component id, or the :godot:`NodePath` itself. Names the node the smoothed pose is written to. A component id resolves through :ref:`display_set_target_item()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_display_set_target_item>`. A :godot:`NodePath` is stored as written, which is what lets a visual be named before the node exists.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **PredictParam**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_ARCHETYPE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_ARCHETYPE** = ``0``

:ref:`Archetype<enum_NetwPredict_Archetype>`. What the entity starts from, such as a character or a vehicle. Set this first and adjust afterwards.

Every :ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` is read and written through :ref:`predict_get_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_get_param>` and :ref:`predict_set_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_set_param>`, writes the like-named member on the entity's :ref:`NetwPredictionHandle<class_NetwPredictionHandle>`, and takes the :godot:`Variant` type named after it below. An archetype sets most of the rest, so a game usually writes one or two.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_SCHEDULE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_SCHEDULE** = ``1``

:ref:`Schedule<enum_NetwPredict_Schedule>`. When in the tick the entity simulates.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_MISSING_POLICY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_MISSING_POLICY** = ``2``

:ref:`MissingInput<enum_NetwPredict_MissingInput>`. What authority does for a tick whose input never arrived.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_RECOVERY_POLICY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_RECOVERY_POLICY** = ``3``

:ref:`RecoveryPolicy<enum_NetwPredict_RecoveryPolicy>`. What the owner does about a divergence, from replaying it to merely reporting it.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_SNAP_RESTORE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_SNAP_RESTORE** = ``4``

:ref:`RestoreMode<enum_NetwPredict_RestoreMode>`. Whether a snap restores to the acknowledged state or extrapolates it forward to now.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_CORRECTION_MODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_CORRECTION_MODE** = ``5``

:ref:`CorrectionMode<enum_NetwPredict_CorrectionMode>`. The mechanism a recovery policy is carried out by.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_TELEPORT_THRESHOLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_TELEPORT_THRESHOLD** = ``6``

:godot:`float` in the property's own units. The fallback distance past which a body is judged to hold nothing worth keeping, used for any property that declares no threshold of its own.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_DIVERGENCE_EPSILON:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_DIVERGENCE_EPSILON** = ``7``

:godot:`float`. The tolerance a divergence is judged against wherever the transition was not declared exactly reproducible.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_BREACH_RESPONSE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_BREACH_RESPONSE** = ``8``

:ref:`BreachResponse<enum_NetwPredict_BreachResponse>`. What happens when a client's claimed state cannot be reconciled at all.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_MAX_RESTORE_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_MAX_RESTORE_TICKS** = ``9``

:godot:`int` ticks. The cap on how far an extrapolated restore may project, so a backlogged acknowledgement cannot launch the body down a long straight line off a curved path.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_COLLISION_COOLDOWN_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_COLLISION_COOLDOWN_TICKS** = ``10``

:godot:`int` ticks. How long after a contact the entity stays out of partial recovery, since a body still being disturbed cannot say which properties are safe to leave behind.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_MAX_CONSUME_PER_TICK:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_MAX_CONSUME_PER_TICK** = ``11``

:godot:`int`. The ceiling on transitions authority replays in one tick, which bounds catch-up cost after a stall.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_MAX_CONSUME_LAG_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_MAX_CONSUME_LAG_TICKS** = ``12``

:godot:`int` ticks. How far behind authority may fall before it stops trying to catch up transition by transition.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_CONSUME_BUFFER_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_CONSUME_BUFFER_TICKS** = ``13``

:godot:`int` ticks. The queue depth authority holds before replaying, the ``buffer`` of :ref:`predict_consume()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_consume>`. Zero by default, so the ordinary verdict is simply whether anything is queued.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_REPLAY_BUFFER_DEPTH:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`PredictParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PredictParam>` **PREDICT_PARAM_REPLAY_BUFFER_DEPTH** = ``14``

:godot:`int`. How many transitions the owner keeps for replay, which bounds how far a correction can rewind.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_IslandParam:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **IslandParam**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_IslandParam>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ISLAND_PARAM_APPROXIMATE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`IslandParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_IslandParam>` **ISLAND_PARAM_APPROXIMATE** = ``0``

:godot:`bool`. Whether the island admits approximate agreement rather than demanding reproducible transitions. An island is the set of entities that must be resimulated together because they interact. Its settings are written through :ref:`predict_island_set_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_island_set_param>`, and :ref:`predict_island_add()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_island_add>` is what puts an entity in one.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ISLAND_PARAM_EXACT_CLAIM:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`IslandParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_IslandParam>` **ISLAND_PARAM_EXACT_CLAIM** = ``1``

:godot:`bool`. Whether the island claims its transitions are exactly reproducible, which is what entitles them to be judged without tolerance.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ISLAND_PARAM_RECONCILE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`IslandParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_IslandParam>` **ISLAND_PARAM_RECONCILE** = ``2``

:ref:`Reconcile<enum_NetwPredict_Reconcile>`. How the island resimulates after a divergence.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ISLAND_PARAM_PROMOTION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`IslandParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_IslandParam>` **ISLAND_PARAM_PROMOTION** = ``3``

:ref:`Promotion<enum_NetwPredict_Promotion>`. What causes a nearby entity to be drawn into the island.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ISLAND_PARAM_PROMOTION_COUNT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`IslandParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_IslandParam>` **ISLAND_PARAM_PROMOTION_COUNT** = ``4``

:godot:`int`. The number of nearest members promoted. Clamped at zero.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ISLAND_PARAM_PROMOTION_METERS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`IslandParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_IslandParam>` **ISLAND_PARAM_PROMOTION_METERS** = ``5``

:godot:`float` meters. The distance that triggers promotion. Clamped at zero.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ISLAND_PARAM_PACING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`IslandParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_IslandParam>` **ISLAND_PARAM_PACING** = ``6``

:ref:`Pacing<enum_NetwPredict_Pacing>`. When the island's members open transitions.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ISLAND_PARAM_INPUT_DELAY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`IslandParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_IslandParam>` **ISLAND_PARAM_INPUT_DELAY** = ``7``

:godot:`int` ticks. The input delay a :ref:`NetwPredict.PACING_DELAY_CLOSED<class_NetwPredict_constant_PACING_DELAY_CLOSED>` group holds. Clamped at zero.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_MemberParam:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **MemberParam**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_MemberParam>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_MEMBER_PARAM_FIDELITY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`MemberParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_MemberParam>` **MEMBER_PARAM_FIDELITY** = ``0``

:ref:`Fidelity<enum_NetwPredict_Fidelity>`. How this member is simulated. A member setting is addressed as ``(entity, member, param)`` through :ref:`predict_island_set_member_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_island_set_member_param>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_MEMBER_PARAM_PREDICTOR:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`MemberParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_MemberParam>` **MEMBER_PARAM_PREDICTOR** = ``1``

:godot:`Callable`. The substituted command producer for this member, or an empty callable for none.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **ColumnType**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_F32:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_F32** = ``0``

32-bit float. Every :ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` is passed to :ref:`schema_add_column()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_schema_add_column>` and says what a value is stored as and how wide it travels.

All of them but :ref:`COLUMN_VARIANT<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_VARIANT>` are a fixed width, which is what makes a column a block of bytes and lets the session compute how many rows fit in a frame. Send anything of a length that varies through :ref:`Netw.channel()<class_Netw_method_channel>` instead.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_F64:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_F64** = ``1``

64-bit float, for a value whose precision is the caller's to decide.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_I8:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_I8** = ``2``

8-bit signed integer.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_U8:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_U8** = ``3``

8-bit unsigned integer.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_I16:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_I16** = ``4``

16-bit signed integer.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_U16:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_U16** = ``5``

16-bit unsigned integer.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_I32:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_I32** = ``6``

32-bit integer.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_I64:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_I64** = ``7``

64-bit integer.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_BOOL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_BOOL** = ``8``

One bit on the wire.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_VECTOR2:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_VECTOR2** = ``9``

:godot:`Vector2`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_VECTOR3:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_VECTOR3** = ``10``

:godot:`Vector3`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_VECTOR4:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_VECTOR4** = ``11``

:godot:`Vector4`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_COLOR:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_COLOR** = ``12``

:godot:`Color`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_QUATERNION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_QUATERNION** = ``13``

:godot:`Quaternion`, distinct from :ref:`COLUMN_VECTOR4<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_VECTOR4>` because smallest-three packing is a semantic rather than a width.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_ENTITY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_ENTITY** = ``14``

A route, the link one table draws to another. Resolve one back to a handle with :ref:`entity_from_route()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_from_route>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_COLUMN_VARIANT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ColumnType<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ColumnType>` **COLUMN_VARIANT** = ``15``

The self-describing tier, what a :godot:`String` or a :godot:`Dictionary` compiles to. Legal in a schema and in a property set, rejected by :ref:`table_create()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_create>`, because variable width has no memcpy and no rows-per-frame budget.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **Stat**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_UNKNOWN_ROUTE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_UNKNOWN_ROUTE** = ``0``

Counter. Frames addressed to a route this peer never saw issued.

Two kinds of value share this enum. :ref:`stats_get()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_stats_get>` takes any of them, and :ref:`stats_snapshot()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_stats_snapshot>` returns the same values under their lowercase names.

A counter only ever rises and says how often something happened, so what matters is how fast it climbs. A gauge says how many there are right now and moves both ways. Every name carrying ``drops`` or ``skips`` counts something the session rejected on purpose, so a rising one points at a cause rather than being a fault itself.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_NOT_LIVE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_NOT_LIVE** = ``1``

Counter. Frames for a route that is lingering or a tombstone. Ordinary during a despawn, since frames already in flight have to land somewhere.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_NO_NODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_NO_NODE** = ``2``

Counter. Frames for a live route whose node is gone.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_BACKLOG_LIMIT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_BACKLOG_LIMIT** = ``3``

Counter. Frames rejected because the receive backlog was already full.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_TRAVERSAL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_TRAVERSAL** = ``4``

Counter. Frames whose node path could not be walked.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_COMP_UNRESOLVED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_COMP_UNRESOLVED** = ``5``

Counter. Frames naming a component id the entity does not have.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_SENDS_DROPPED_UNROUTABLE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_SENDS_DROPPED_UNROUTABLE** = ``6``

Counter. Outbound frames with no route to send them on.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_SENDS_DROPPED_NOT_LIVE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_SENDS_DROPPED_NOT_LIVE** = ``7``

Counter. Outbound frames for an entity that stopped being live before the send.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_SYNC_DROPS_STALE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_SYNC_DROPS_STALE** = ``8``

Counter. Sync frames older than what has already been applied. Expected under reordering, and the reason stamps travel.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DERIVED_SETS_ACTIVE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DERIVED_SETS_ACTIVE** = ``9``

Gauge. Derived property sets currently registered.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DERIVED_FRAMES_IN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DERIVED_FRAMES_IN** = ``10``

Counter. Derived frames received.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_DERIVED_NO_SET:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_DERIVED_NO_SET** = ``11``

Counter. Derived frames naming a set this peer has not compiled.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_DERIVED_BAD_SENDER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_DERIVED_BAD_SENDER** = ``12``

Counter. Derived frames from a peer not entitled to author them.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_DERIVED_SCHEMA:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_DERIVED_SCHEMA** = ``13``

Counter. Derived frames whose schema hash disagrees with the local set. A rising count means the two builds compiled different declarations.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_ROW_FRAMES_OUT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_ROW_FRAMES_OUT** = ``14``

Counter. Per-recipient declared-set row frames sent.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_ROW_FRAMES_FULL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_ROW_FRAMES_FULL** = ``15``

Counter. Row frames that carried every column rather than a diff, which is what a peer gets before its baseline is established.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_ROW_FRAMES_STAGE_REFUSED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_ROW_FRAMES_STAGE_REFUSED** = ``16``

Counter. Row frames the installed encode stage rejected. A pass that sent nothing because its stage rejected everything otherwise reads exactly like a caught-up one.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_ROW_FRAMES_UNGATHERED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_ROW_FRAMES_UNGATHERED** = ``17``

Counter. Rows whose gather rejected, so no recipient was offered one.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_RETAINED_FRAMES_OUT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_RETAINED_FRAMES_OUT** = ``18``

Counter. Per-recipient retained row frames sent on the reliable lane. Rises only when a retained column changed for that recipient, so a flat count beside a rising :ref:`STAT_ROW_FRAMES_OUT<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_ROW_FRAMES_OUT>` is the healthy reading rather than a stalled lane.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_WINDOW_FRAMES_OUT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_WINDOW_FRAMES_OUT** = ``19``

Counter. Windowed row frames sent, one per recipient per pass.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_WINDOW_SAMPLES_OUT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_WINDOW_SAMPLES_OUT** = ``20``

Counter. Ticks those frames carried in total. It exceeds :ref:`STAT_WINDOW_FRAMES_OUT<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_WINDOW_FRAMES_OUT>` by the redundancy the window buys, so the two together read as the cost of healing without a retransmit.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_SYNC_SETS_ACTIVE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_SYNC_SETS_ACTIVE** = ``21``

Gauge. Sync property sets currently registered.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_SYNC_FRAMES_OUT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_SYNC_FRAMES_OUT** = ``22``

Counter. Full sync frames sent.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_SYNC_FRAMES_IN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_SYNC_FRAMES_IN** = ``23``

Counter. Full sync frames received.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DELTA_FRAMES_OUT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DELTA_FRAMES_OUT** = ``24``

Counter. Delta sync frames sent.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DELTA_FRAMES_IN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DELTA_FRAMES_IN** = ``25``

Counter. Delta sync frames received.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_SYNC_NO_SET:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_SYNC_NO_SET** = ``26``

Counter. Sync frames naming a set this peer has not compiled.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_SYNC_BAD_SENDER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_SYNC_BAD_SENDER** = ``27``

Counter. Sync frames from a peer the set's :ref:`NetwPropertySet.policy<class_NetwPropertySet_property_policy>` does not admit.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_SYNC_POISONED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_SYNC_POISONED** = ``28``

Counter. Sync frames rejected because the stream's baseline is no longer trustworthy, so a delta cannot be applied against it.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_DROPS_SYNC_UNKNOWN_FLAG:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_DROPS_SYNC_UNKNOWN_FLAG** = ``29``

Counter. Sync frames carrying a flag bit this build does not know.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_SPAWN_BOOK_ARMED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_SPAWN_BOOK_ARMED** = ``30``

Gauge. Entities armed for spawn but not yet sent.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_SPAWN_BOOK_SPAWNED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_SPAWN_BOOK_SPAWNED** = ``31``

Counter. Entities spawned out to peers.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_SPAWN_BOOK_RECV:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_SPAWN_BOOK_RECV** = ``32``

Counter. Spawns received and materialized locally.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_SENT_PACKETS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_SENT_PACKETS** = ``33``

Counter. Packets handed to the carrier.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_SENT_BYTES:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_SENT_BYTES** = ``34``

Counter. Bytes handed to the carrier, framing included.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_RECEIVED_PACKETS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_RECEIVED_PACKETS** = ``35``

Counter. Packets taken from the carrier.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_RECEIVED_BYTES:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_RECEIVED_BYTES** = ``36``

Counter. Bytes taken from the carrier, framing included.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_STATE_ACKS_OUT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_STATE_ACKS_OUT** = ``37``

Counter. State acknowledgements sent, which is how a peer's delta baseline advances.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_STATE_ACKS_IN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_STATE_ACKS_IN** = ``38``

Counter. State acknowledgements received.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_STANDALONE_ACKS_OUT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_STANDALONE_ACKS_OUT** = ``39``

Counter. Acknowledgements sent on their own because no state frame was going out to carry them.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_PENDING_LIVE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant
